\subsection{Specifying a Business Use Case (BUC)}
\label{sec:buc}

Each business use case below is the work's response to the business event
with the same number. It describes the whole response, including the parts
done by people, and names the product use cases (Table~\ref{tab:puc}) that
are Squigglr's part of it.

\begin{description}[style=nextline]
\item[BUC-001 Add a child (BE-001)] The adult creates a profile for the
child and chooses each adult-set value, using the example drawings for
\texttt{STAGE\_PASS}, or leaves the defaults (BR-008). The work records
the profile and the values. Product use case: \ucref{001}.
\item[BUC-002 Choose the next practice (BE-002)] The adult judges what
the child is ready for, from knowing the child and, once there is
practice, from the records (ASM-006). The adult then chooses a lesson and
its starting stage. Product use cases: \ucref{002}, \ucref{004}.
\item[BUC-003 Practise a guided lesson (BE-003)] The adult starts the
lesson. The child watches the demonstration and draws an attempt at each
stage. The work checks each attempt against the stage's criteria, gives
feedback, and moves the child on or offers a retry. The lesson ends when
the child finishes it or reaches \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT}, or
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT\_MAX} when extended, and every attempt is recorded
(BR-002).
Product use cases: \ucref{003}, \ucref{008}.
\item[BUC-004 Respond to repeated difficulty (BE-004)] The work suggests
going back a stage. The adult decides whether to accept; a declined or
ignored suggestion changes nothing (BR-003). Product use case:
\ucref{005}.
\item[BUC-005 Draw freely (BE-005)] The child draws and edits in the
creative space. The work refines the drawing a little and keeps it
editable. The child or the adult can turn refinement off or restore the
original (BR-005; SRC-009.01). Product use cases: \ucref{006}, \ucref{007}.
\item[BUC-006 Review progress (BE-006)] The adult reads a summary of the
child's practice, opens the attempts behind it, and uses what they see
to decide on further practice (BUC-002). The work gives no diagnosis or
judgement of the child (BR-006). Product use case: \ucref{009}.
\item[BUC-007 Change a child's settings (BE-007)] The adult opens the
child's history or settings and changes accessibility, audio or
adult-set values. The work accepts only values within their ranges
(BR-008). Product use case: \ucref{010}.
\item[BUC-008 Maintain lessons and ranges (BE-008)] The team adds or
changes lessons, or changes the ranges of the adult-set values, using
adult-made drawings (BR-009). Squigglr has no product use case for this
event; the maintenance requirements cover it.
\end{description}

\section{Business Data Model and Data Dictionary}
\label{sec:data-model}

This section defines the business data that Squigglr creates, uses and
keeps: what a supporting adult's account, a learner profile, a lesson,
a lesson record, an attempt record and a creative drawing are, how they relate, and what
each term in the requirements means. The model is
conceptual. Following the Volere template~\cite{robertson2012volere}, it
records the subject matter the business must remember and the rules that hold between its parts; it
is not a database design, and it fixes no tables, keys, storage or
technology. The names defined here are the names the functional
requirements use, in particular those of
Sections~\ref{sec:fr-creative}, \ref{sec:fr-records} and
\ref{sec:fr-progress}, and the product use cases UC-001 to UC-010
(Table~\ref{tab:puc}) create, read and change this data.
Subsection~\ref{sec:bdm} gives the model as a diagram and states the data
constraints. Subsection~\ref{sec:data-dictionary} defines every class,
attribute and data flow.

\subsection{Business Data Model}
\label{sec:bdm}

Figure~\ref{fig:data-model} shows the seven classes of business data that
Squigglr keeps and how they relate. A supporting adult's account holds
learner profiles, and each learner profile has its own learner settings
(BR-001, BR-008). The team maintains the lessons, each with its four
stages and their predefined difficulties (BR-009; LC-001 in
Table~\ref{tab:likelyChanges}). Each time an adult starts a lesson for a
learner, Squigglr keeps a new lesson record, which holds the attempt
records and any go-back suggestions of that lesson (G.3, G.5). The
creative space keeps creative drawings, each with its strokes and
refinements (G.4, BR-005). A progress summary is not a class: Squigglr
derives it from one learner's lesson and attempt records whenever an adult
opens it (G.6), so Table~\ref{tab:data-dictionary} defines it as a data
flow. That table also gives the attributes of each class and how many of
one class relate to another.

\begin{figure}[htbp]
\centering
\begin{tikzpicture}[x=4.6cm, y=-2cm,
  cls/.style={draw, rectangle, minimum width=3.2cm, text width=3.05cm,
    minimum height=1cm, align=center, font=\small},
  rel/.style={thick},
  lbl/.style={font=\scriptsize, fill=white, inner sep=1.5pt}]
\node[cls] (acc) at (1,0) {Supporting\\adult's account};
\node[cls] (lp) at (1,1) {Learner profile};
\node[cls] (ls) at (2,1) {Learner settings};
\node[cls] (cd) at (0,2) {Creative drawing};
\node[cls] (lr) at (1,2) {Lesson record};
\node[cls] (les) at (2,2) {Lesson};
\node[cls] (ar) at (1,3) {Attempt record};
\draw[rel] (acc) -- node[lbl] {holds} (lp);
\draw[rel] (lp) -- node[lbl] {has} (ls);
\draw[rel] (lp) -- node[lbl] {has} (lr);
\draw[rel] (lp) -| node[lbl, pos=0.25] {has} (cd);
\draw[rel] (lr) -- node[lbl] {is for} (les);
\draw[rel] (lr) -- node[lbl] {holds} (ar);
\end{tikzpicture}
\caption{Business data model of Squigglr. Each box is a class of business
data, defined with its attributes in Table~\ref{tab:data-dictionary}. Each
line is a relationship between two classes. Its label reads from the box
nearer the top of the figure to the other box, or, for two boxes side by
side, from the left box to the right one.}
\label{fig:data-model}
\end{figure}

The model carries the following data constraints. None is new; each
restates as a property of the data a rule or goal stated elsewhere, and
each can be checked by inspecting saved data prepared by adults.
\begin{itemize}
  \item A learner profile holds only the child's first name and a
  username that does not identify the child (BR-001).
  \item Every adult-set value lies within its team-set range,
  \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT} is larger than \texttt{DIFFICULTY\_STREAK},
  and \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT\_MAX} is at least \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT}; a
  value the adult does not choose is the middle of its range (BR-008).
  \item Within one lesson record, the evaluable attempts at one stage
  never exceed \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT\_MAX} (BR-002). An interrupted
  attempt, or one whose input is invalid or too ambiguous to evaluate,
  is not an evaluable attempt and is not counted (FR-LRN-009,
  FR-LRN-010).
  \item The actual strokes of an attempt record are the strokes the child
  drew; they are never replaced by a corrected, ideal or refined shape
  (BR-004, BR-005).
  \item Every observed difficulty of an attempt record is a predefined
  difficulty of that attempt's stage, and a later correction adds a mark
  without removing the difficulty (G.5, BR-007).
  \item A refinement keeps every refined stroke within
  \texttt{REFINE\_TOLERANCE} of the original stroke, and it changes
  neither the number nor the order, colours and widths of the strokes
  (G.4, BR-005; FR-CRT-011, FR-CRT-012).
  \item The original strokes of a refinement never change, so a restore
  returns them exactly (G.4, BR-005).
  \item A creative drawing is never an attempt record and never counts in
  a lesson record or a progress summary (BR-004; FR-CRT-028).
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Data Dictionary}
\label{sec:data-dictionary}

Table~\ref{tab:data-dictionary} defines every class of
Figure~\ref{fig:data-model}, every attribute of a class and every data
flow that enters or leaves the work. For a class, the Unit, domain or
constraint column also states how it relates to the other classes. Three
flows share the name of the data they carry:
F5 is the refined drawing, F7 the lesson choice and
F10 the go-back suggestion. Entries are sorted by type and then
by name. The Meaning column uses the composition notation of the Volere
template: ``A = B + C'' means that A is made of B and C; ``\{B\}'' means
any number of B; ``[B \textbar{} C]'' means exactly one of B or C; and
``(B)'' means that B may be absent. Units and domains are stated at the
business level, and every bound uses a named constant rather than a
number; most are in Table~\ref{tab:goalconst}. The Used by column lists
the goals, business rules (Section~\ref{sec:business-rules}), use cases
and requirements that use each entry; F1 to F12 are the data flows of
Table~\ref{tab:work-flows}. Older names
from earlier documents are given in the Meaning column where they help a
reader.

{\footnotesize
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{3pt}
\begin{longtable}{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.13\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.29\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.105\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.21\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash\exhyphenpenalty=10000}%
    p{0.185\textwidth}@{}}
\caption{Data dictionary}\label{tab:data-dictionary}\\
\toprule
\textbf{Name} & \textbf{Meaning} & \textbf{Type} &
\textbf{Unit, domain or constraint} & \textbf{Used by} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Name} & \textbf{Meaning} & \textbf{Type} &
\textbf{Unit, domain or constraint} & \textbf{Used by} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endlastfoot
Attempt record & The record of one attempt, which is one try at one
drawing stage of a lesson. Attempt record = Stage + Actual strokes +
\{Observed difficulty\} + [Outcome \textbar{} Interrupted mark] + (Stage
result) + Attempt end time + Adult-set values in use. & Class &
One for each attempt that ends with an outcome, or that an adult
interrupts after at least one stroke; belongs to exactly one lesson
record. An interrupted attempt does not count towards an attempt limit.
& G.5; UC-008; FR-LRN-009; FR-REC-001 to FR-REC-009; FR-PRG-006 \\
\addlinespace
Creative drawing & One drawing in the creative space. Creative drawing =
\{Stroke\} + \{Refinement\} + Refined state. & Class &
Belongs to the learner profile that was the active learner when it was
drawn; never an attempt record. & G.4; BR-005; UC-006, UC-007;
FR-CRT-001 to FR-CRT-030 \\
\addlinespace
Learner profile & One child known to an account (called a child's
profile in UC-001 and F6). Learner profile = First name + Username. &
Class &
Holds nothing else about the child; has exactly one learner settings and
any number of lesson records and creative drawings; only the adult who
holds the account creates, views and manages it. & BR-001; ASM-008;
UC-001; F6; FR-REC-006; FR-CRT-023 \\
\addlinespace
Learner settings & The values that tailor practice to one learner.
Learner settings = Adult-set values + Audio setting + Accessibility
settings + Refinement setting. & Class &
Exactly one for each learner profile; changed by the adult who holds the
account, except that the child can turn refinement off in the creative
space (BUC-005). & BR-008; ASM-015; UC-001, UC-007, UC-010; F2, F6,
F9; NFR-UX-010, NFR-UX-011 \\
\addlinespace
Lesson & A catalogue item, such as drawing a square, that teaches one
line, shape or simple drawing through stages that give gradually less
help (called an activity in earlier documents). Lesson = Lesson name +
\{Stage + \{Predefined difficulty\}\}. & Class &
Exactly four stages, in the order given under Stage; maintained by the
team; any number of lesson records are for it. & G.1; ASM-010; BR-009;
LC-001; FR-LRN-001; UC-002, UC-003; F12 \\
\addlinespace
Lesson record & The record of one start of a lesson by a learner, up to
the lesson's end. Lesson record = Lesson + Starting stage + Furthest
stage reached + Successful attempts per stage + Extension count + Lesson
ending + \{Attempt record\} + \{Go-back suggestion\}. & Class &
Belongs to exactly one learner profile and is for exactly one lesson; a
new lesson record each time the learner starts the lesson; unchanged
after the lesson ends, except by deletion. & G.5; BR-002; UC-003;
FR-REC-006 to FR-REC-010; FR-PRG-002; SAF-RAY-006 \\
\addlinespace
Supporting adult's account & The account of a parent, guardian,
caregiver, educator or learning-support staff member who uses Squigglr
with one or more children. Supporting adult's account = \{Learner
profile\}. & Class &
Held by one adult, who alone sees and manages its learner profiles and
their records; how the adult is identified is left to the Access
Requirements. & BR-001; ASM-009; ULC-002 \\
\addlinespace
Accessi\-bility settings & The accessibility options chosen for a
learner. & Attribute &
Content to be set by the Accessibility Requirements. & UC-010; F9 \\
\addlinespace
Active learner & The learner profile that an adult has selected for the
current session, to which attempts, suggestions and creative drawings
are saved. & Attribute &
One learner profile of the account in use. & ASM-008; FR-CRT-023,
FR-CRT-026; SAF-RAY-007 \\
\addlinespace
Actual strokes & The strokes the child drew in an attempt. Actual
strokes = \{Stroke\}. & Attribute &
In the order drawn, as captured; never replaced by a corrected, ideal
or refined shape. & BR-004; ULC-005; FR-LRN-007; FR-REC-002; FR-CRT-027 \\
\addlinespace
Adult-set values & The six values a supporting adult chooses for each
child (Table~\ref{tab:goalconst}). Adult-set values =
\texttt{STAGE\_PASS} + \texttt{DIFFICULTY\_STREAK} +
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT} + \texttt{IMPROVE\_WINDOW} +
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_EXTENSION} + \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT\_MAX}. & Attribute &
Each within its team-set range, by default its middle;
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT} larger than
\texttt{DIFFICULTY\_\allowbreak STREAK};
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT\_\allowbreak MAX} at least
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT}. \texttt{STAGE\_PASS} is a result in
points out of 100; the others are counts of attempts. & G.2, G.3; BR-008;
FR-LRN-017; UC-001, UC-010; F6, F9; SAF-RAY-008 \\
\addlinespace
Adult-set values in use & The learner's adult-set values at the time an
attempt was made. & Attribute &
As for adult-set values; a later change to the learner settings does
not alter them. & ASM-006; LC-007; FR-REC-004; FR-PRG-006 \\
\addlinespace
Attempt end time & When an attempt ended. & Attribute &
Date and time. & FR-REC-001; FR-PRG-002, FR-PRG-011 \\
\addlinespace
Audio setting & Whether feedback to the learner includes audio. &
Attribute & On or muted. & ASM-015; UC-010; F4, F9; NFR-UX-010 \\
\addlinespace
Colour & The colour of a stroke in a creative drawing. & Attribute &
One of the colours Squigglr offers; unchanged by refinement. &
FR-CRT-005, FR-CRT-011 \\
\addlinespace
Correction mark & Whether the child corrected an observed difficulty
later in the same attempt (called a change during the attempt in
earlier documents). & Attribute &
Corrected or not corrected; a correction never removes the observed
difficulty. & G.5; FR-REC-003; FR-PRG-004 \\
\addlinespace
Extension count & The number of times the attempt limit of a lesson
record was extended because the child was improving. & Attribute &
Count of extensions, at most one at each stage; evaluable attempts at
one stage never exceed
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT\_\allowbreak MAX}. & BR-002;
FR-LRN-020; SAF-RAY-006 \\
\addlinespace
First name & The child's first name, as the adult enters it. &
Attribute & First name only; no surname or other detail. & BR-001;
F6 \\
\addlinespace
Furthest stage reached & The latest stage of the lesson that the learner
reached in a lesson record. & Attribute &
A stage of the lesson. & G.5; BR-002; FR-REC-006; FR-PRG-002 \\
\addlinespace
Go-back suggestion & A suggestion that the learner go back to an earlier
stage, which an adult accepts or declines (called a difficulty suggestion
in FR-LRN-019). Go-back suggestion = Suggested stage + Suggestion
status. & Attribute &
Part of exactly one lesson record; made only after
\texttt{DIFFICULTY\_\allowbreak STREAK} failed attempts in a row at one
stage, and not while the child is improving; changes the stage only if an
adult accepts it; a declined or ignored suggestion is made again only
after another full streak. & G.3; BR-003; CON-007; UC-005; F8, F10;
FR-LRN-019; FR-REC-010; SAF-RAY-001, SAF-RAY-007 \\
\addlinespace
Interrupted mark & Marks an attempt that an adult stopped, by ending the
session or switching learner, after at least one stroke and before its
outcome was decided. & Attribute &
Set or not set; an interrupted attempt has no outcome. & ASM-008,
ASM-015; FR-REC-005; FR-PRG-009 \\
\addlinespace
Latest attempt time & The attempt end time of the most recent attempt
that a progress summary includes. & Attribute &
Date and time. & FR-PRG-011 \\
\addlinespace
Lesson ending & How the lesson of a lesson record ended. & Attribute &
In progress; every stage passed; ended at
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT}; ended when a granted extension ran
out or \texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT\_\allowbreak MAX} was
reached; or unfinished, when an adult stopped it early. & BR-002;
FR-LRN-021; FR-REC-007; FR-PRG-002; SAF-RAY-005, SAF-RAY-006 \\
\addlinespace
Lesson name & The name of a lesson in the catalogue, such as drawing a
square. & Attribute &
Names one lesson of the catalogue. & G.1; LC-001 \\
\addlinespace
Observed difficulty & A predefined difficulty that Squigglr observed in
an attempt. Observed difficulty = Predefined difficulty + Correction
mark. & Attribute &
A predefined difficulty of the attempt's stage, listed at most once in
each attempt record. & G.5; BR-007; FR-REC-003; FR-PRG-004; F4 \\
\addlinespace
Original strokes & The strokes of a creative drawing exactly as they
were immediately before a refinement: the same strokes in the same
order, with the same points, colours and widths. For the first
refinement of a drawing, they are the child's own strokes. Original
strokes = \{Stroke\}. & Attribute &
At least one stroke; never changed, so that a restore returns them
exactly. & G.4; BR-005; FR-CRT-017 to FR-CRT-019, FR-CRT-023 \\
\addlinespace
Outcome & Whether an attempt passed: its drawing is complete and its
result meets the \texttt{STAGE\_PASS} in use (passed, also called
successful), or it does not (not passed, also called unsuccessful or
failed). & Attribute &
Passed or not passed; absent for an interrupted attempt. & G.2; BR-002;
FR-LRN-013; FR-REC-001; F4 \\
\addlinespace
Path & The points of a stroke, in the order drawn. Path =
\{Point\}. & Attribute &
At least one point. & ASM-004; FR-LRN-007; SAF-AHM-002; FR-REC-002; FR-CRT-011, FR-CRT-012 \\
\addlinespace
Point & One position on the drawing area that a stroke passes
through. & Attribute &
Position on the drawing area, in the same unit of distance as
\texttt{REFINE\_\allowbreak TOLERANCE}; the unit is TBD and will be fixed
with the value of that constant. & ASM-004; FR-CRT-012 \\
\addlinespace
Practice-only statement & The statement on every progress summary that
it describes practice only and is not an assessment or
diagnosis. & Attribute &
Fixed text, shown on every summary. & BR-006; SAF-RAY-004 \\
\addlinespace
Predefined difficulty & A problem that the team defines in advance and
that Squigglr can observe in an attempt, such as a missed part of an
outline (called an observation in earlier documents). & Attribute &
Defined by the team for one or more stages; never generated for a
particular child. & G.5; BR-007; FR-REC-003; FR-PRG-004 \\
\addlinespace
Recurring difficulty & A predefined difficulty that a progress summary
lists because it keeps occurring. Recurring difficulty = Predefined
difficulty + Stage + Lesson + count of attempts + count
corrected. & Attribute &
Observed in at least \texttt{RECUR\_MIN} attempts at the same stage of
the same lesson; \texttt{RECUR\_MIN} is a team-set count, TBD, to be
fixed with the other team-set values (OI-05). & G.6; FR-PRG-004 \\
\addlinespace
Refined drawing & A creative drawing while a refinement of it is in
effect: the strokes the refinement produced, as the child may later
edit them. & Attribute &
Same number, order, colours and widths of strokes as its original
strokes; every refined stroke within
\texttt{REFINE\_\allowbreak TOLERANCE} of its original stroke; stays
editable. & G.4; BR-005; FR-CRT-011 to FR-CRT-013; F5 \\
\addlinespace
Refined state & Whether a creative drawing holds a refinement that has
not been undone or restored. & Attribute &
Refined or not refined. & FR-CRT-016 \\
\addlinespace
Refinement & One automatic, minimal adjustment of the strokes of a
creative drawing, made when the child finishes the drawing. Refinement =
Original strokes + Refinement status. & Attribute &
Part of exactly one creative drawing and concerns one supported item;
applied whole or not at all; only in the creative space, never to a
guided lesson. & G.4; BR-005; UC-007; FR-CRT-010 to FR-CRT-021 \\
\addlinespace
Refinement setting & Whether automatic refinement is on for a learner in
the creative space. & Attribute &
On or off. & BR-005; UC-007, UC-010; F2, F9; FR-CRT-030; NFR-UX-011 \\
\addlinespace
Refinement status & Where one refinement stands. & Attribute &
In effect, undone or restored. & FR-CRT-007, FR-CRT-017 \\
\addlinespace
Stage & One of the four steps of a lesson, which give gradually less
help (also called a guidance level in Table~\ref{tab:glossary}).
Attempts at the second and third stages are made with a guide and at the
fourth without one (FR-LRN-012 calls them assisted and independent). &
Attribute &
Demonstration; tracing a full outline; drawing between endpoints only;
or drawing with no guide, in that order. Attempts are made only at the
three drawing stages, not at the demonstration. & G.1, G.2, G.6; BR-002;
FR-LRN-002, FR-LRN-003, FR-LRN-012; FR-PRG-003; \texttt{STAGE\_PASS} \\
\addlinespace
Stage result & The result of an evaluable attempt on the normalized scale
of Table~\ref{tab:goalconst}, where higher results indicate closer
agreement with the stage's fixed criteria. & Attribute &
Points out of 100; absent for an interrupted attempt. & BR-002; ASM-006;
LC-008; FR-LRN-013, FR-LRN-020; FR-REC-001 \\
\addlinespace
Starting stage & The stage at which a learner starts a lesson. &
Attribute &
A stage of the lesson, chosen by an adult. & BR-003; UC-004;
FR-LRN-016; FR-REC-006; FR-PRG-010 \\
\addlinespace
Stroke & One continuous line drawn with the stylus. Stroke = Path +
(Colour + Width). & Attribute &
Belongs to exactly one attempt record, creative drawing or refinement;
colour and width are kept for strokes in creative drawings. & CON-003;
ASM-004; BR-004; FR-REC-002; FR-CRT-001; F1 \\
\addlinespace
Successful attempts per stage & For each stage of a lesson record, the
number of attempts that passed. Successful attempts per stage =
\{Stage + count of attempts\}. & Attribute &
Count of attempts. & G.5; BR-002; FR-REC-006; FR-PRG-002 \\
\addlinespace
Suggested stage & The stage a go-back suggestion proposes. & Attribute &
An earlier stage of the same lesson than the one at which the failed
attempts were made; on acceptance the adult chooses the earlier stage. &
BR-003; UC-005; FR-LRN-019 \\
\addlinespace
Suggestion status & Where a go-back suggestion stands. & Attribute &
Pending, accepted, declined or ignored; only an adult accepts or
declines, and a declined or ignored suggestion changes nothing. & G.3;
BR-003; UC-005; F8; FR-LRN-019; FR-REC-010 \\
\addlinespace
Supported item & A shape or simple object in the agreed supported set,
which Squigglr refines. & Attribute &
The set is agreed by the team (OI-03); drawings of other items are not
refined. & G.4; FR-CRT-015 \\
\addlinespace
Username & A name for the learner that the adult chooses, so that
learners under one account can be told apart. & Attribute &
Must not identify the child. & BR-001; ASM-008; F6 \\
\addlinespace
Width & The width of a stroke in a creative drawing. & Attribute &
Unchanged by refinement. & FR-CRT-011 \\
\addlinespace
Attempt record view & What the adult sees for one attempt record. Attempt
record view = every value of the attempt record, with the actual strokes
as recorded. & Data flow &
Shown only to the adult who holds the account. & ULC-002; UC-009,
UC-010; FR-PRG-006; F11 \\
\addlinespace
Catalogue update & What the team adds or changes. Catalogue update =
\{Lesson\} + \{Supported item\} + (Team-set values). & Data flow &
Made from adult-made drawings only. & BR-009; LC-001, LC-002; F12 \\
\addlinespace
Child navigation & How a child moves through Squigglr. Child
navigation = [Open a lesson \textbar{} Restart \textbar{} Move back and
forth \textbar{} Leave]. & Data flow &
Never changes the lesson choice or the starting stage. & FACT-007;
BR-003 \\
\addlinespace
Editing action & A change the child makes to the open creative drawing.
Editing action = [Select \textbar{} Move \textbar{} Resize \textbar{}
Recolour \textbar{} Erase \textbar{} Undo \textbar{} Redo] + Part. Part
= \{Stroke\}, the strokes the child selects. & Data flow &
Acts only on the open creative drawing. & G.4; FR-CRT-002 to
FR-CRT-008 \\
\addlinespace
Feedback & What Squigglr tells the child about an attempt. Feedback =
\{Observed difficulty\} + Outcome. & Data flow &
Drawn only from predefined difficulties, never generated advice; spoken
only when the audio setting is on. & G.1; BR-007; ASM-015; FR-LRN-014; F4 \\
\addlinespace
Finish mark & The child's mark that a creative drawing is done. & Data
flow &
Starts exactly one refinement. & UC-007; FR-CRT-010 \\
\addlinespace
Learner profile details & What an adult enters to add a child. Learner
profile details = First name + Username + (Adult-set values). & Data
flow &
From the adult who holds the account only; an adult-set value not
entered takes the middle of its range. & BR-001, BR-008; UC-001; F6 \\
\addlinespace
Learner selection & An adult's choice of the active learner on a
device. Learner selection = Learner profile. & Data flow &
One learner profile of the account in use. & ASM-008; SAF-RAY-007 \\
\addlinespace
Lesson choice & The lesson and starting stage that an adult chooses for
a learner; starting the lesson begins a new lesson record. Lesson choice
= Lesson + Starting stage. & Data flow &
Made only by an adult; the starting stage is a stage of the chosen
lesson. & BR-003; CON-007; FR-LRN-016; UC-002 to UC-004; F7;
SAF-RAY-001 \\
\addlinespace
Progress summary & A view of one learner's practice over time.
Progress summary = Learner profile + \{Lesson record\} + counts of
attempts made with a guide + counts of attempts made without a guide +
\{Recurring difficulty\} + Latest attempt time + Practice-only
statement. & Data flow &
Derived only from one learner's records, with no data of its own; no
overall score; the two kinds of attempt are never added together. &
G.6; BR-006; UC-009; FR-PRG-001 to FR-PRG-012; F11 \\
\addlinespace
Refinement off request & A child's request, in the creative space, to
turn refinement off. & Data flow &
Made in the creative space only; never reaches a guided lesson. & BR-005;
UC-007; F2; FR-CRT-030 \\
\addlinespace
Refinement result & The adjusted strokes returned for one refinement by
the service that refines drawings (LC-004). Refinement result =
\{Path\}, one for each stroke of the drawing. & Data flow &
Applied whole only if it meets FR-CRT-011 and FR-CRT-012; otherwise the
drawing is left unchanged. & LC-004; FR-CRT-020, FR-CRT-021 \\
\addlinespace
Restore request & A request to restore the original strokes of a
creative drawing. & Data flow &
From the child for the open drawing, or from the adult who holds the
account for any saved drawing. & BR-005; F2, F9; FR-CRT-017 to
FR-CRT-019 \\
\addlinespace
Settings change & An adult's change to a learner's settings. Settings
change = (Adult-set values) + (Audio setting) + (Accessibility
settings) + (Refinement setting). & Data flow &
From the adult who holds the account only; values outside their ranges
are refused. & BR-008; UC-010; F9; SAF-RAY-008 \\
\addlinespace
Stage guide & What Squigglr shows the child at a stage. Stage guide =
[Demonstration \textbar{} Full outline \textbar{} Endpoints]. & Data
flow &
Set by the stage; none at the stage with no guide. & G.1; F3 \\
\addlinespace
Strokes drawn & The strokes a child draws for an attempt or in the
creative space. Strokes drawn = \{Stroke\}. & Data flow &
Positions and order of points as the browser reports them. & CON-003;
ASM-004; FR-LRN-007; SAF-AHM-001; F1 \\
\addlinespace
Suggestion response & An adult's answer to a go-back suggestion.
Suggestion response = [Accept \textbar{} Decline]. & Data flow &
From an adult only. & BR-003; UC-005; F8; FR-LRN-018; FR-REC-010; SAF-RAY-001 \\
\addlinespace
Summary view choice & What an adult asks a progress summary to show.
Summary view choice = \{Lesson\} + Date range + \{Recorded
value\}. & Data flow &
Changes only what is shown, never the records; until the adult chooses,
the summary covers all records. & ASM-006; LC-007; FR-PRG-007 \\
\end{longtable}
}

\section{The Scope of the Product}
\label{sec:product-scope}

This section describes Squigglr's own part of the work in
Section~\ref{sec:work-scope}: the inputs and outputs that cross the
product's boundary, the product use cases, and the full flow of each use
case.

\subsection{Product Boundary}
\label{sec:product-boundary}

Table~\ref{tab:product-inputs} lists every input Squigglr accepts and
Table~\ref{tab:product-outputs} every output it produces, covering the
inputs and outputs in the Problem Statement and Goals (SRC-001) and the
data flows in Table~\ref{tab:work-flows}. Each row names the use cases
(Table~\ref{tab:puc}) and requirements that handle it. For each input,
the last column states what Squigglr does when the input is invalid or
missing, so that every possible input has a defined response. An
identifier marked TBD names a requirement group that is not written yet;
the owner's issue is given.

{\footnotesize
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{4pt}
\begin{longtable}{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.19\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.13\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.1\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.18\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.3\textwidth}@{}}
\caption{Product inputs}\label{tab:product-inputs}\\
\toprule
\textbf{Input} & \textbf{From} & \textbf{Use cases} &
\textbf{Requirements} & \textbf{If invalid or missing} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Input} & \textbf{From} & \textbf{Use cases} &
\textbf{Requirements} & \textbf{If invalid or missing} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endlastfoot
A child's profile details and adult-set values & Supporting adult &
\ucref{001}, \ucref{010} &
\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-017}}, SAF-RAY-008 &
A value outside its range, an \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT} not larger than
\texttt{DIFFICULTY\_STREAK}, or an \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT\_MAX} smaller
than \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT} is not saved. A value the adult does not
choose is set to the middle of its range (SAF-RAY-008). \\
\addlinespace
Lesson choice, starting stage and the start of the lesson &
Supporting adult & \ucref{002}, \ucref{004} &
\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-016}}, \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-018}}, SAF-RAY-001 &
Without an adult's choice no lesson starts; a child cannot choose or
start one by tapping icons (SAF-RAY-001). On an unsupported device or
browser, the adult sees a notice that results may be unreliable
(SAF-RAY-002). \\
\addlinespace
Stylus strokes for an attempt in a guided lesson & Child &
\ucref{003}, \ucref{008} &
\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-007}}, \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-008}} &
An interrupted or unfinished attempt is not passed and does not count
toward the attempt limits (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-009}}). Missing, invalid or
ambiguous input is not passed and is not counted
(\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-010}}). \\
\addlinespace
Strokes and edits in the creative space: draw, move, resize, recolour,
erase, undo & Child & \ucref{006} &
FR-CRT TBD (\#83) &
To be stated with the FR-CRT requirements. \\
\addlinespace
Request to turn refinement off or restore the original drawing &
Child or supporting adult & \ucref{007}, \ucref{010} &
FR-CRT TBD (\#83); BR-005 &
A failed refinement leaves the drawing unchanged (\goalref{4}). \\
\addlinespace
Decision on a suggestion to go back a stage & Supporting adult &
\ucref{005} &
\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-018}}, \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-019}}, SAF-RAY-001 &
A declined or ignored suggestion changes nothing, and the suggestion
stays pending without blocking practice until an adult decides
(SAF-RAY-001; BR-003). Only an adult can accept it. \\
\addlinespace
Changes to a child's settings: accessibility, audio, whether refinement
is on & Supporting adult & \ucref{010} &
NFR-UX-010, NFR-UX-011 &
A setting changes only for the child it was changed for (NFR-UX-010,
NFR-UX-011); changes need the adult check (SAF-RAY-008). \\
\addlinespace
Request to view a child's history, attempts or progress summaries &
Supporting adult & \ucref{009}, \ucref{010} &
BR-001; NFR-SEC TBD (\#82) &
A request from anyone other than the adult who holds the account is
refused, and nothing is shown (BR-001). \\
\addlinespace
Lesson catalogue, ranges of the adult-set values and fixed values &
Squigglr team & -- &
\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-001}}, BR-009 &
Not entered by users; set and checked by the team (BR-009). \\
\addlinespace
Any of the above while the network connection is lost & Child or
supporting adult & All &
SAF-RAY-003 &
Each completed attempt is kept on the device until the server confirms
it is saved, and the adult is told which attempts could not be saved
(SAF-RAY-003). \\
\end{longtable}
}

{\footnotesize
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{4pt}
\begin{longtable}{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.38\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.14\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.14\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.27\textwidth}@{}}
\caption{Product outputs}\label{tab:product-outputs}\\
\toprule
\textbf{Output} & \textbf{To} & \textbf{Use cases} &
\textbf{Requirements} \\

\textbf{Requirements} \\

\subsection{Product Use Case Table}
\label{sec:puc-table}

Table~\ref{tab:puc} lists the ten product use cases, UC-001 to UC-010.
Each row names the actor who starts or carries out the use case, the
trigger that starts it, what the actor wants when it ends (the user goal)
and the project goals it serves (Table~\ref{tab:goals}). Squigglr is
named as the actor where the product starts the use case itself. The
identifiers are fixed and never renumbered; requirements trace to them,
and Section~\ref{sec:puc} gives each use case's full flows. Names such as
\texttt{STAGE\_PASS} are named constants defined in the constants register
(Table~\ref{tab:goalconst}).

{\footnotesize
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{4pt}
\begin{longtable}{@{}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.09\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.2\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.25\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.29\textwidth}
  >{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{0.08\textwidth}@{}}
\caption{Product use cases}\label{tab:puc}\\
\toprule
\textbf{ID} & \textbf{Actor: use case} & \textbf{Trigger} &
\textbf{User goal} & \textbf{Goals} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{ID} & \textbf{Actor: use case} & \textbf{Trigger} &
\textbf{User goal} & \textbf{Goals} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endlastfoot
\phantomsection\label{uc:001}%
UC-001 & \emph{Supporting adult:} create a child's profile &
The adult adds a child to Squigglr. &
The child's profile exists, and every adult-set value is within its
range, or at the middle of the range if the adult did not choose one. &
\goalref{3} \\
\addlinespace
\phantomsection\label{uc:002}%
UC-002 & \emph{Supporting adult:} choose a lesson &
The adult chooses a lesson for a child. &
The chosen lesson is ready for the adult to start. & \goalref{1},
\goalref{3} \\
\addlinespace
\phantomsection\label{uc:003}%
UC-003 & \emph{Child:} practise a guided lesson &
An adult starts a lesson for the child. &
The child moves to the next stage after an attempt meets
\texttt{STAGE\_PASS}, or retries; the lesson ends when the child finishes
it or reaches \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT}, or \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT\_MAX}
when extended (BR-002), and the furthest stage reached is saved.
& \goalref{1}, \goalref{2}, \goalref{3} \\
\addlinespace
\phantomsection\label{uc:004}%
UC-004 & \emph{Supporting adult:} start the child at a later stage &
The adult judges that the child needs less help in a lesson. &
The lesson starts at the stage the adult chose. & \goalref{3} \\
\addlinespace
\phantomsection\label{uc:005}%
UC-005 & \emph{Supporting adult:} respond to a suggestion to go back a stage &
Squigglr suggests an earlier stage after \texttt{DIFFICULTY\_STREAK}
failed attempts in a row. &
The stage changes only if the adult accepts; a declined or ignored
suggestion changes nothing. & \goalref{3} \\
\addlinespace
\phantomsection\label{uc:006}%
UC-006 & \emph{Child:} create and edit artwork &
The child opens the creative space. &
The child's drawing is saved and stays editable. & \goalref{4} \\
\addlinespace
\phantomsection\label{uc:007}%
UC-007 & \emph{Squigglr:} refine artwork and restore the original &
The child finishes a drawing in the creative space. &
The drawing is refined within \texttt{REFINE\_TOLERANCE} and stays
editable; refinement can be turned off; the original strokes can be
restored; a failed refinement leaves the drawing unchanged. & \goalref{4} \\
\addlinespace
\phantomsection\label{uc:008}%
UC-008 & \emph{Squigglr:} record an attempt &
The child ends an attempt at a lesson stage. &
The record keeps the stage, every predefined difficulty observed, later
corrections and the outcome. & \goalref{5} \\
\addlinespace
\phantomsection\label{uc:009}%
UC-009 & \emph{Supporting adult:} review progress &
The adult opens a child's progress summary. &
The adult sees the child's progress over time, with attempts made
without a guide separated from attempts made with one, and can open the
attempts behind it. & \goalref{6} \\
\addlinespace
\phantomsection\label{uc:010}%
UC-010 & \emph{Supporting adult:} review history and change settings &
The adult opens a child's history or settings. &
Only an authorised adult sees the history; the accessibility and audio
settings, whether refinement is on, and the adult-set values are changed,
and each adult-set value stays within its range. & \goalref{3}, \goalref{6} \\
\end{longtable}
}

\subsection{Individual Product Use Cases (PUC's)}
\label{sec:puc}

% Long identifiers and constant names cannot be hyphenated; let lines
% stretch rather than overflow the margin in this subsection.
\begingroup
\sloppy
This subsection gives the full flow of each product use case in
Table~\ref{tab:puc}. Each use case lists its actor, preconditions,
trigger, main flow, alternative flows, failure flows and postconditions,
and the requirements and sources it depends on. Alternative flows are
other normal paths; failure flows handle the invalid or missing inputs in
Table~\ref{tab:product-inputs}. Both are numbered by the main flow step
where they branch, so ``3a'' branches at step 3. The lesson behaviour in
\ucref{003} and \ucref{005} is specified formally in
Section~\ref{sec:fr-formal}. An identifier marked TBD names a
requirement group that is not written yet; the owner's issue is given.

\subsubsection{UC-001: Create a child's profile}
\label{puc:001}
\reqfield{Actor}{Supporting adult who holds the account.}
\reqfield{Preconditions}{The adult is signed in to their own account
(BR-001).}
\reqfield{Trigger}{The adult adds a child to Squigglr.}
\noindent\textit{Main flow:}
\begin{enumerate}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em]
  \item The adult enters the child's first name and a username that does
  not identify the child (BR-001).
  \item Squigglr asks for each adult-set value, shows its range and, for
  \texttt{STAGE\_PASS}, shows example drawings of the minimum and maximum
  (BR-008).
  \item The adult chooses each value.
  \item Squigglr saves the profile and the values.
\end{enumerate}
\noindent\textit{Alternative flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[3a.] The adult leaves a value unset. Squigglr sets it to the
  middle of its range (\mbox{SAF-RAY-008}).
\end{description}
\noindent\textit{Failure flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[3b.] A value is outside its range, \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT} is not
  larger than \texttt{DIFFICULTY\_STREAK}, or \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT\_MAX}
  is smaller than \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT}. Squigglr does not save it and
  returns to step 3 (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-017}}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-008}).
  \item[4a.] The profile cannot be saved. Squigglr tells the adult, and no
  partial profile is created.
\end{description}
\reqfield{Postconditions}{The profile exists, and every adult-set value is
within its range or at its midpoint.}
\reqfield{Requirements}{\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-017}}; \mbox{SAF-RAY-008}; BR-001,
BR-008.}
\reqfield{Sources}{\goalref{3}; \mbox{SRC-008}.}

\subsubsection{UC-002: Choose a lesson}
\label{puc:002}
\reqfield{Actor}{Supporting adult.}
\reqfield{Preconditions}{The child has a profile (\ucref{001}).}
\reqfield{Trigger}{The adult chooses a lesson for a child.}
\noindent\textit{Main flow:}
\begin{enumerate}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em]
  \item The adult selects the child, and Squigglr shows that child as the
  active learner (\mbox{SAF-RAY-007}).
  \item The adult chooses a lesson from the catalogue
  (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-001}}).
  \item Squigglr checks the device and browser against
  \texttt{SUPPORTED\_DEVICES} (\mbox{SAF-RAY-002}).
  \item The adult completes the adult check and starts the lesson at its
  first stage (\mbox{SAF-RAY-001}, \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-016}}). The lesson
  continues in \ucref{003}.
\end{enumerate}
\noindent\textit{Alternative flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[4a.] The adult chooses a later starting stage (\ucref{004}).
\end{description}
\noindent\textit{Failure flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[3a.] The device or browser is not supported. The adult sees a
  notice that results may be unreliable and decides whether to continue
  (\mbox{SAF-RAY-002}).
  \item[4b.] A child tries to choose or start a lesson by tapping icons.
  The adult check is not completed, and no lesson starts (\mbox{SAF-RAY-001}).
\end{description}
\reqfield{Postconditions}{The chosen lesson is running for the selected
child.}
\reqfield{Requirements}{\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-001}},
\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-016}}, \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-018}}; \mbox{SAF-RAY-001},
\mbox{SAF-RAY-002}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-007}.}
\reqfield{Sources}{\goalref{1}, \goalref{3}; \mbox{SRC-005.01}.}

\subsubsection{UC-003: Practise a guided lesson}
\label{puc:003}
\reqfield{Actor}{Child.}
\reqfield{Preconditions}{An adult has started the lesson for the child
(\ucref{002} or \ucref{004}).}
\reqfield{Trigger}{An adult starts a lesson for the child.}
\noindent\textit{Main flow:}
\begin{enumerate}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em]
  \item Squigglr shows the demonstration, which is not an attempt
  (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-003}}), then moves to the starting stage.
  \item Squigglr shows the stage's guidance: the full outline, the
  endpoints only, or no guide
  (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-004}}--\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-006}}).
  \item The child draws one or more strokes. Lifting the stylus does not
  end the attempt (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-007}},
  \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-008}}).
  \item When the attempt is finalized, Squigglr classifies it as complete
  or incomplete and as assisted or independent, and passes it only if it
  is complete and meets \texttt{STAGE\_PASS}
  (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-011}}--\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-013}}).
  \item Squigglr shows pre-approved feedback about the drawing, never
  about the child (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-014}},
  \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-022}}), and records the attempt (\ucref{008}).
  \item On a pass, Squigglr moves to the next stage and repeats from
  step 2. A pass at independent drawing completes the lesson
  (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-023}}).
\end{enumerate}
\noindent\textit{Alternative flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[6a.] The attempt does not pass and the stage's limit is not
  reached. The child retries from step 3 (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-015}}).
  \item[6b.] The child has \texttt{DIFFICULTY\_STREAK} non-passing
  attempts in a row and is not improving. Squigglr offers the adult a
  suggestion to go back a stage (\ucref{005}); practice continues
  meanwhile (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-019}}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-001}).
  \item[6c.] The unsuccessful attempts reach \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT}
  while the child is improving. Squigglr allows up to
  \texttt{ATTEMPT\_EXTENSION} more attempts, never more than
  \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT\_MAX} in total, and the child continues from
  step 3 (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-020}}).
\end{description}
\noindent\textit{Failure flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[3a.] The attempt is interrupted or unfinished. It is not passed
  and does not count toward the limits (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-009}}).
  \item[3b.] The input is missing, invalid, too ambiguous to evaluate, or
  cannot be mapped reliably to the drawing surface. It is not passed and
  does not count (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-010}}, \mbox{SAF-AHM-002}).
  \item[3c.] The same stylus event is reported more than once. It counts
  once (\mbox{SAF-AHM-001}).
  \item[6d.] The unsuccessful attempts reach \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT}
  without an extension, an extension is used up, or the total reaches
  \texttt{ATTEMPT\_LIMIT\_MAX}. The lesson ends with no action from the
  child, and the progress so far is saved (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-021}},
  \mbox{SAF-RAY-005}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-006}).
  \item[*a.] At any step, the connection is lost. Each completed attempt
  is kept on the device until the server confirms it is saved, and the
  adult is told which attempts could not be saved (\mbox{SAF-RAY-003}).
\end{description}
\reqfield{Postconditions}{Every evaluable attempt is recorded, the
furthest stage reached is saved, and no stage moved back without an
adult's acceptance.}
\reqfield{Requirements}{\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-002}}--\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-015}},
\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-019}}--\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-023}}; \mbox{SAF-RAY-003},
\mbox{SAF-RAY-005}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-006}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-007}; \mbox{SAF-AHM-001},
\mbox{SAF-AHM-002}.}
\reqfield{Sources}{\goalref{1}, \goalref{2}, \goalref{3}; \mbox{SRC-005.01}.}

\subsubsection{UC-004: Start the child at a later stage}
\label{puc:004}
\reqfield{Actor}{Supporting adult.}
\reqfield{Preconditions}{The child has a profile (\ucref{001}).}
\reqfield{Trigger}{The adult judges that the child needs less help in a
lesson, from knowing the child or from the records (\ucref{009}).}
\noindent\textit{Main flow:}
\begin{enumerate}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em]
  \item The adult selects the child and the lesson, as in \ucref{002}
  steps 1 to 3.
  \item The adult chooses a later starting stage.
  \item The adult completes the adult check (\mbox{SAF-RAY-001}).
  \item Squigglr starts the lesson at that stage, with the stage's counts
  at zero (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-016}}; Section~\ref{sec:fr-formal}).
  The lesson continues in \ucref{003}.
\end{enumerate}
\noindent\textit{Alternative flows:} None.
\par
\noindent\textit{Failure flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[3a.] A child tries to change the starting stage. The adult check
  is not completed, and the stage does not change (\mbox{SAF-RAY-001},
  \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-018}}).
\end{description}
\reqfield{Postconditions}{The lesson starts at the stage the adult chose.}
\reqfield{Requirements}{\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-016}},
\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-018}}; \mbox{SAF-RAY-001}; BR-003.}
\reqfield{Sources}{\goalref{3}; \mbox{SRC-005.01}.}

\subsubsection{UC-005: Respond to a suggestion to go back a stage}
\label{puc:005}
\reqfield{Actor}{Supporting adult.}
\reqfield{Preconditions}{A lesson is running (\ucref{003}), and the child
has \texttt{DIFFICULTY\_STREAK} non-passing attempts in a row at an
eligible stage without improving.}
\reqfield{Trigger}{Squigglr suggests going back a stage
(\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-019}}).}
\noindent\textit{Main flow:}
\begin{enumerate}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em]
  \item Squigglr shows the suggestion and keeps it pending without
  blocking practice (\mbox{SAF-RAY-001}).
  \item The adult completes the adult check and accepts.
  \item Squigglr moves the lesson back one stage, with the stage's counts
  at zero (Section~\ref{sec:fr-formal}).
\end{enumerate}
\noindent\textit{Alternative flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[2a.] The adult declines. Nothing changes, and a new suggestion
  needs a new full streak (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-019}}).
  \item[2b.] The adult does not respond. The suggestion stays pending and
  nothing changes (\mbox{SAF-RAY-001}, BR-003).
\end{description}
\noindent\textit{Failure flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[2c.] A child tries to accept the suggestion. The adult check is
  not completed, and nothing changes (\mbox{SAF-RAY-001}).
\end{description}
\reqfield{Postconditions}{The stage has changed only if an adult
accepted.}
\reqfield{Requirements}{\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-018}}--\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-020}};
\mbox{SAF-RAY-001}; BR-003.}
\reqfield{Sources}{\goalref{3}; \mbox{SRC-005.01}.}

\subsubsection{UC-006: Create and edit artwork}
\label{puc:006}
\reqfield{Actor}{Child.}
\reqfield{Preconditions}{The child is the active learner (\mbox{SAF-RAY-007}).}
\reqfield{Trigger}{The child opens the creative space.}
\noindent\textit{Main flow:}
\begin{enumerate}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em]
  \item The child draws strokes on the canvas.
  \item The child edits the drawing: moves, resizes, recolours, erases
  or undoes parts of it (\goalref{4}).
  \item Squigglr saves the drawing, which stays editable. Refinement runs
  as in \ucref{007}.
\end{enumerate}
\noindent\textit{Alternative flows:} None.
\par
\noindent\textit{Failure flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[2a.] An edit cannot be applied. The drawing stays as it was.
  \item[3a.] The drawing cannot be saved. The child keeps drawing, and
  the adult is told that it was not saved.
\end{description}
\reqfield{Postconditions}{The drawing is saved and stays editable.}
\reqfield{Requirements}{FR-CRT TBD (\#83).}
\reqfield{Sources}{\goalref{4}; \mbox{SRC-009.01}.}

\subsubsection{UC-007: Refine artwork and restore the original}
\label{puc:007}
\reqfield{Actor}{Squigglr; the child or the supporting adult restores
the original or turns refinement off.}
\reqfield{Preconditions}{A drawing exists in the creative space
(\ucref{006}), and refinement is on.}
\reqfield{Trigger}{The child finishes a drawing in the creative space.}
\noindent\textit{Main flow:}
\begin{enumerate}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em]
  \item Squigglr refines the child's own strokes, moving no point more
  than \texttt{REFINE\_TOLERANCE} (BR-005, \goalref{4}).
  \item The refined drawing stays editable (\ucref{006}).
\end{enumerate}
\noindent\textit{Alternative flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[2a.] The child or the adult restores the original. Squigglr
  returns the original strokes exactly (\goalref{4}, BR-005).
  \item[2b.] The child or the adult turns refinement off. Later drawings
  are not refined (\mbox{SRC-009.01}).
\end{description}
\noindent\textit{Failure flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[1a.] Refinement fails. The drawing is left unchanged
  (\goalref{4}).
\end{description}
\reqfield{Postconditions}{The drawing is refined or unchanged, stays
editable, and its original can be restored. Refinement never reaches a
guided lesson record (BR-004, BR-005).}
\reqfield{Requirements}{FR-CRT TBD (\#83); BR-004, BR-005.}
\reqfield{Sources}{\goalref{4}; \mbox{SRC-009.01}.}

\subsubsection{UC-008: Record an attempt}
\label{puc:008}
\reqfield{Actor}{Squigglr.}
\reqfield{Preconditions}{A lesson is running for an identified learner
(\ucref{003}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-007}).}
\reqfield{Trigger}{The child ends an attempt at a lesson stage.}
\noindent\textit{Main flow:}
\begin{enumerate}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em]
  \item Squigglr records the child's actual strokes, never a corrected
  shape (BR-004), with the stage, the guidance and completeness
  classifications, every predefined difficulty observed, later
  corrections and the outcome (\goalref{5};
  \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-011}}, \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-012}}).
  \item Squigglr saves the record to the active learner only
  (\mbox{SAF-RAY-007}).
  \item The server confirms the record is saved.
\end{enumerate}
\noindent\textit{Alternative flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[1a.] The attempt ends the lesson at a limit, or an extension is
  granted. Squigglr also records that event (\mbox{SAF-RAY-006}).
\end{description}
\noindent\textit{Failure flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[1b.] The attempt is interrupted or its input is invalid. It is
  not recorded as passed and does not count
  (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-009}}, \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-010}}).
  \item[1c.] A stylus event is reported more than once. It is recorded
  once (\mbox{SAF-AHM-001}).
  \item[3a.] The server does not confirm. The record stays on the device
  until it is saved, and the adult is told which attempts could not be
  saved (\mbox{SAF-RAY-003}).
\end{description}
\reqfield{Postconditions}{The record matches what the child drew and
holds no score, diagnosis or comparison (\mbox{SAF-RAY-004}).}
\reqfield{Requirements}{FR-REC TBD (\#83);
\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-009}}--\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-012}}; \mbox{SAF-RAY-003},
\mbox{SAF-RAY-004}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-006}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-007}; \mbox{SAF-AHM-001}.}
\reqfield{Sources}{\goalref{5}; \mbox{SRC-001}.}

\subsubsection{UC-009: Review progress}
\label{puc:009}
\reqfield{Actor}{Supporting adult who holds the account.}
\reqfield{Preconditions}{The adult is signed in to their own account.}
\reqfield{Trigger}{The adult opens a child's progress summary.}
\noindent\textit{Main flow:}
\begin{enumerate}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em]
  \item Squigglr shows the child's progress over time, with attempts
  made without a guide separated from attempts made with one
  (\goalref{6}), the adult-set values in use (\mbox{SAF-RAY-008}), and a
  statement that the summary describes practice only and is not an
  assessment (\mbox{SAF-RAY-004}).
  \item The adult opens an attempt behind the summary.
  \item Squigglr shows that attempt's record (\ucref{008}).
\end{enumerate}
\noindent\textit{Alternative flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[1a.] The child has no practice yet. Squigglr says so.
  \item[1b.] Lessons ended at a limit or were extended. Squigglr shows
  how many times for each lesson (\mbox{SAF-RAY-006}).
\end{description}
\noindent\textit{Failure flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[1c.] The request comes from anyone other than the account holder.
  It is refused, and nothing is shown (BR-001).
  \item[1d.] Some attempts are not saved yet. The adult is told which
  (\mbox{SAF-RAY-003}).
\end{description}
\reqfield{Postconditions}{The adult has seen the child's progress;
nothing is changed.}
\reqfield{Requirements}{FR-PRG TBD (\#83); \mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-022}};
\mbox{SAF-RAY-003}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-004}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-006}, \mbox{SAF-RAY-008};
BR-001, BR-006.}
\reqfield{Sources}{\goalref{6}; \mbox{SRC-005.02}.}

\subsubsection{UC-010: Review history and change settings}
\label{puc:010}
\reqfield{Actor}{Supporting adult who holds the account.}
\reqfield{Preconditions}{The adult is signed in to their own account, and
the child has a profile (\ucref{001}).}
\reqfield{Trigger}{The adult opens a child's history or settings.}
\noindent\textit{Main flow:}
\begin{enumerate}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em]
  \item Squigglr shows the child's history and current settings.
  \item The adult changes the accessibility or audio settings, whether
  refinement is on, or the adult-set values.
  \item The adult completes the adult check (\mbox{SAF-RAY-008}).
  \item Squigglr saves the changes.
\end{enumerate}
\noindent\textit{Alternative flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[2a.] The adult restores the original of a creative drawing
  (\ucref{007}).
\end{description}
\noindent\textit{Failure flows:}
\begin{description}[nosep, leftmargin=2.2em, font=\normalfont]
  \item[1a.] The request comes from anyone other than the account holder.
  It is refused, and nothing is shown (BR-001).
  \item[3a.] A child tries to change a setting. The adult check is not
  completed, and nothing changes (\mbox{SAF-RAY-008}).
  \item[4a.] An adult-set value is outside its range or inconsistent with
  the others. It is not saved (\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-017}},
  \mbox{SAF-RAY-008}).
\end{description}
\reqfield{Postconditions}{The changes are saved, and every adult-set
value is within its range.}
\reqfield{Requirements}{\mbox{\reqref{FR-LRN-017}}; \mbox{SAF-RAY-008};
NFR-UX-010, NFR-UX-011; NFR-SEC TBD (\#82); BR-001, BR-008.}
\reqfield{Sources}{\goalref{3}, \goalref{6}; \mbox{SRC-008}, \mbox{SRC-009.01}.}
\endgroup

\section{Functional Requirements}
\label{sec:functional}
Functional requirements are grouped by externally observable product
behaviour. Each requirement uses the identifier, fit criterion and
traceability conventions defined in the Introduction.

\subsection{Learning Activity Requirements}
\label{sec:fr-learning}

This subsection specifies the externally observable behaviour of guided
lessons. It builds on G.1--G.3, CON-003, CON-007 and BR-002--BR-009,
with source statements in Appendix~\ref{app:sources}.
The core catalogue covers lines, shapes and simple drawings; letter tracing
is deferred (ASM-010, LC-001). Delivery phase identifiers follow the
conventions in the Introduction and the phase plan in
Section~\ref{sec:tasks}.

\paragraph{Progression within lessons and adult control.}
A supporting adult selects each lesson and its starting stage and authorizes
skips or backward changes (G.3, CON-007). Within the selected lesson, a
passing drawing attempt automatically advances to the next guidance stage
(G.2, BR-002). Passing the independent stage completes the current lesson;
Squigglr does not automatically select another lesson.

\paragraph{Lesson content and guidance.}
\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-001}{Approved lesson catalogue}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall offer guided lessons for the approved catalogue of lines,
basic shapes and simple drawings, excluding letter tracing activities from
the core catalogue.}
  \reqrationale{These are the agreed categories for structured practice; letters are deferred
(G.1, ASM-010). The lesson catalogue is maintained separately from the stage rules
(LC-001).}
  \reqfit{Inspect the lesson catalogue and verify that every approved
catalogue entry is selectable, classified as a line, shape or simple drawing,
and has the stages in FR-LRN-002; verify that no letter activity is required
to complete the core catalogue.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.1; \srcref{001}; \hyperref[src:009.04]{SRC-009.04}}
  \reqtraces{ASM-010; LC-001; \ucref{002}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-002}{Ordered guidance stages}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall make the following ordered guidance stages available for each
guided lesson: demonstration, full outline tracing, endpoint-only guidance
and independent drawing.}
  \reqrationale{The activity described to specialists removes guidance in this order
(G.1--G.2).}
  \reqfit{For every approved lesson, inspect all four stages in the specified order;
confirm that the stage chosen by an adult is the stage presented when
practice begins.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.1--G.2; \srcref{001}; \srcref{005}}
  \reqtraces{BR-003; \ucref{003}; \ucref{004}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-003}{Demonstration}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall show an example of the intended drawing during a lesson's
demonstration stage without treating that demonstration as a learner attempt.}
  \reqrationale{A demonstration provides an example before guided practice and must not
inflate the learner's records.}
  \reqfit{Start each approved lesson in its demonstration stage; verify that the
intended drawing can be viewed and that watching it adds no learner attempt
or passed stage record.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.1; \srcref{001}; \srcref{005}}
  \reqtraces{BR-004; \ucref{003}; \ucref{008}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-004}{Full-outline guidance}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall display the lesson's complete reference outline during the
full-outline tracing stage and accept stylus strokes drawn against it.}
  \reqrationale{Outline tracing is the first guided drawing stage in the approved
progression.}
  \reqfit{For each lesson, enter the full-outline stage, verify that the complete
reference shape is visible, and draw an attempt using stylus input.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.1; \srcref{001}; \srcref{005}}
  \reqtraces{CON-003; \ucref{003}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-005}{Endpoint-only guidance}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall show the specified start and end points, without the full
reference outline, during the endpoint-only stage and accept stylus strokes
between those points.}
  \reqrationale{The middle drawing stage reduces visual assistance while retaining the
destination cues.}
  \reqfit{For each lesson, enter endpoint-only guidance; verify that the relevant
endpoints are shown, the full outline is absent, and stylus drawing is
accepted.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.1--G.2; \srcref{001}; \srcref{005}}
  \reqtraces{CON-003; \ucref{003}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-006}{Independent drawing}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall permit the drawing task to be attempted in the independent
stage without displaying tracing outlines or endpoint guides.}
  \reqrationale{The final stage needs to be distinguishable from assisted practice (G.2,
G.6).}
  \reqfit{Enter the independent stage for each lesson; verify that neither an outline
nor endpoint guides appear and that an attempt can still be made using the
stylus.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.2; \srcref{001}}
  \reqtraces{BR-004; \ucref{003}; \ucref{008}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\paragraph{Input, classification and feedback.}
\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-007}{Stylus stroke acquisition}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall accept stylus-drawn strokes as ordered sequences of input
points for the active lesson attempt and preserve their order and stroke
boundaries for subsequent classification.}
  \reqrationale{Stage checks and faithful attempt records require the original stroke
structure (ASM-004, BR-004).}
  \reqfit{Supply known one-stroke and multi-stroke stylus samples; verify that the
captured point order, number of strokes and boundaries match the samples.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.2, G.5; \srcref{001}; \srcref{004}}
  \reqtraces{CON-003; ASM-004; BR-004; \ucref{003}; \ucref{008}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-008}{Multi-stroke attempts}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall evaluate the collected strokes of a single attempt together
rather than classify each stylus lift as a completed attempt.}
  \reqrationale{Lines and shapes can be composed of multiple strokes, and lifting a stylus
alone does not establish that the task is finished.}
  \reqfit{For a prepared two-stroke figure, lift the stylus after the first stroke and
draw the second; confirm that one attempt is evaluated against the complete
figure, not two independently completed attempts.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-2}
  \reqsources{G.1--G.2; ASM-004; \srcref{001}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-007; \ucref{003}; \ucref{008}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-009}{Interrupted attempt handling}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall distinguish an interrupted or unfinished drawing attempt from
a completed drawing attempt, shall not record it as passed, and shall not
consume an evaluable attempt from the stage limit.}
  \reqrationale{An input interruption or unfinished drawing is not evidence of a correctly
completed task.}
  \reqfit{Interrupt known partial attempts between and during strokes; verify that none
is reported as passed, that the active stage does not advance, and that
unfinished attempts are distinguishable from finalized attempts and that
stage attempt counters are unchanged.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-2}
  \reqsources{G.2, G.5; ASM-004; \srcref{001}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-008; BR-004; \ucref{003}; \ucref{008}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-010}{Invalid and ambiguous input}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall withhold passing status from an attempt whose required stylus
input is missing, invalid or too ambiguous to evaluate against the stage
criteria, and shall not count such input toward the stage's evaluable-attempt
or unsuccessful attempt limits.}
  \reqrationale{Unreliable acquisition must not silently turn a malformed drawing into a
successful result (ASM-004).}
  \reqfit{Submit empty input, input with missing coordinate data and data for which the
intended stroke order cannot be established; confirm that none passes,
that a non-success status is available, and that the stage's attempt
counters are unchanged.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.2; ASM-004; BR-004; \srcref{001}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-007; \ucref{003}; H-AHM-001; H-AHM-003}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-011}{Completeness classification}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall classify every evaluable drawing attempt as complete or
incomplete relative to the selected lesson and guidance stage.}
  \reqrationale{Stage success must distinguish an unfinished line or missing side from a
completed drawing (G.2).}
  \reqfit{Evaluate adult-made or prepared examples with every required component
present and with a deliberately missing side, segment or required endpoint;
verify complete and incomplete classifications match the known examples.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.2; BR-002; \srcref{001}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-013; \ucref{003}; \ucref{008}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-012}{Guidance classification}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall classify each evaluated attempt as assisted when performed
with outline or endpoint guidance and independent when performed without
those guides.}
  \reqrationale{Adults need to distinguish guided practice from practice without a guide
(G.6).}
  \reqfit{Perform an evaluable attempt in each drawing stage; verify that outline and
endpoint attempts are marked assisted and no-guide attempts are marked
independent.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-2}
  \reqsources{G.2, G.6; \srcref{001}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-004--006; \ucref{008}; \ucref{009}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-013}{Pass classification}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall report an evaluable attempt as passing its stage only when the
drawing is classified as complete and its result meets the current learner's
\texttt{STAGE\_\allowbreak PASS} criterion.}
  \reqrationale{A pass evaluates the submitted drawing against defined criteria, not the
child's ability (G.2, BR-006).}
  \reqfit{For prepared complete and incomplete drawings whose results lie below, at and
above the adult-selected threshold, verify that only complete drawings
meeting the threshold pass.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.2; BR-002; BR-006; \srcref{008}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-010--011; \ucref{003}; H-AHM-004}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-014}{Predefined difficulty feedback}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall present only pre-approved, stage-relevant feedback
observations for evaluated attempts, including applicable missed tracing
portions, incomplete lines or shapes and incorrect endpoints.}
  \reqrationale{Feedback must describe observable drawing difficulties and not generate
personalized AI advice (BR-007).}
  \reqfit{For each defined feedback category, evaluate a prepared matching drawing and
verify that the corresponding approved observation appears; verify that no
free-generated advice or diagnosis is shown.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-2}
  \reqsources{G.1--G.2; BR-007; \srcref{001}; \hyperref[src:009.02]{SRC-009.02}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-011; \ucref{003}; H-AHM-005}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-015}{Retry after non-passing attempts}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall allow the learner to retry a drawing stage following an
attempt that does not pass, provided the stage has not reached its
applicable attempt limit.}
  \reqrationale{G.1 requires opportunities to practise again without treating failure as a
permanent stage result.}
  \reqfit{Present a non-passing, evaluable sample before the limit and verify that
another attempt can be started at the same drawing stage; repeat at the limit
and verify that the lesson-end rule is followed instead.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.1--G.3; BR-002; \srcref{001}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-021; \ucref{003}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\paragraph{Adult control, suggestions and stopping rules.}
\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-016}{Adult lesson and starting stage selection}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall use the lesson and starting guidance stage selected by the
supporting adult for the learner's session, including an adult-selected later
starting stage.}
  \reqrationale{The adult controls initial level selection rather than the product assigning
readiness (G.3, CON-007).}
  \reqfit{Select each available starting stage as an adult, launch the lesson, and
verify that the selected stage is used; check that a child-only practice
action does not change the configured lesson or starting stage.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.3; CON-007; \hyperref[src:005.01]{SRC-005.01}; \srcref{006}}
  \reqtraces{BR-003; \ucref{002}; \ucref{004}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-017}{Adult-selected practice parameters}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall apply the learner's adult-selected values for
\texttt{STAGE\_\allowbreak PASS}, \texttt{DIFFICULTY\_\allowbreak STREAK}, \texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT},
\texttt{IMPROVE\_\allowbreak WINDOW}, \texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak EXTENSION} and
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT\_\allowbreak MAX}, using the documented midpoint default for any
unset value and rejecting out-of-range or inconsistent selections.}
  \reqrationale{BR-008 requires learner-specific settings and rejects contradictory limit
relationships.}
  \reqfit{Test every setting at the minimum, default and maximum values in
Table~\ref{tab:goalconst}, plus one value outside each bound; verify that
selected values are used, defaults are applied correctly, and invalid
combinations are rejected.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-2}
  \reqsources{G.2--G.3; BR-008; \srcref{008}}
  \reqtraces{OI-05; \ucref{001}; \ucref{010}; H-AHM-007}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-018}{Adult authorization for discretionary changes}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall require explicit authorization from the supporting
adult before changing to another lesson, skipping a guidance stage or moving
backward to an earlier stage; routine pass-based advancement to the next
stage of the current lesson requires no additional adult action.}
  \reqrationale{Adults retain control of lesson selection and nonroutine guidance
changes (G.3, CON-007), without interrupting the agreed forward progression
on passing attempts (G.2, BR-002).}
  \reqfit{Try selecting a new lesson, skipping ahead and applying a return
suggestion without adult authorization, then repeat with authorization;
verify that only authorized discretionary changes take effect. Verify
independently that a passing attempt advances within the selected lesson
without an additional adult action (FR-LRN-023).}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-2}
  \reqsources{G.2--G.3; CON-007; BR-002--003; \hyperref[src:005.01]{SRC-005.01}}
  \reqtraces{OI-04 (TA confirmation); FR-LRN-023; \ucref{004}; \ucref{005};
H-AHM-006}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-019}{Difficulty suggestion}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall offer, without applying, an optional suggestion to return to
an earlier drawing stage when the learner reaches
\texttt{DIFFICULTY\_\allowbreak STREAK} consecutive non-passing evaluable attempts
at an eligible stage and the improvement rule does not suppress suggestions.
When an adult declines or ignores the suggestion, another suggestion shall
require a new full failure streak.}
  \reqrationale{A supporting adult should decide whether earlier guidance is appropriate
(G.3, BR-003).}
  \reqfit{Replay known failing sequences immediately below and at the selected streak
threshold at both eligible stages, including sequences with fewer than
twice the configured \texttt{IMPROVE\_WINDOW} results and sequences with qualifying
improvement; verify that insufficient history does not delay the first
suggestion, that qualifying improvement suppresses it, and that a declined
suggestion reappears only after another full failure streak.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-2}
  \reqsources{G.3; BR-003; \hyperref[src:005.01]{SRC-005.01}; \srcref{008}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-018; FR-LRN-020; \ucref{005}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-020}{Improvement-based extension}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall suppress a difficulty suggestion while the learner
qualifies as improving, and, when the unsuccessful-attempt count first reaches
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT}, grant up to
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak EXTENSION} additional evaluable stage attempts if the average
result of the most recent \texttt{IMPROVE\_\allowbreak WINDOW} attempts exceeds the average
of the preceding equally sized window by at least
\texttt{IMPROVE\_\allowbreak MARGIN}, subject to
\texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT\_\allowbreak MAX}. The extension shall be granted at most
once per stage and expire after the allotted additional evaluable attempts,
even if results continue to improve.}
  \reqrationale{The agreed improvement rule reduces premature interruption while retaining a
hard cap (G.3, BR-002).}
  \reqfit{Evaluate prepared improving and non-improving sequences exactly at the margin
boundary and at the applicable limit; verify extension and suggestion
suppression only for qualifying improvement, exactly the permitted
number of extension attempts without regranting, and no attempt beyond
the maximum.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-2}
  \reqsources{G.3; BR-002--003; \srcref{001}; \srcref{008}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-019; FR-LRN-021; OI-05; \ucref{003}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-021}{Stage attempt limit and lesson end}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall end the active lesson when its unsuccessful-attempt count
reaches \texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT} without a qualifying extension,
or a granted extension is exhausted, or the allowed total-attempt count
reaches \texttt{ATTEMPT\_\allowbreak LIMIT\_\allowbreak MAX}, and make
the furthest stage reached available for the lesson record.}
  \reqrationale{The limits prevent indefinite repetition and preserve usable progress (G.3,
G.5, BR-002).}
  \reqfit{Replay sequences at the base limit and at the maximum, with and without
qualifying improvement; verify that a granted extension expires after
the allotted attempts, does not renew, and is capped by the maximum.
Verify that the highest stage reached is included in the saved lesson outcome.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-2}
  \reqsources{G.3, G.5; BR-002; \srcref{001}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-015; FR-LRN-020; \ucref{003}; \ucref{008}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-022}{No learner evaluation or diagnosis}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall express lesson feedback and completion results as properties
of the drawing attempt rather than as assessments, diagnoses or comparisons
of children.}
  \reqrationale{The product is a practice tool, not an assessment instrument (BR-006).}
  \reqfit{Inspect all guided lesson feedback and completion outcomes, using prepared
attempts; verify that each refers to the task, stage or observed strokes and
that none assigns a diagnosis, ability label or inter-child ranking.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.2; BR-006; \srcref{001}; \srcref{008}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-013--014; \ucref{003}; \ucref{009}}
\end{srsrequirement}

\begin{srsrequirement}{FR-LRN-023}{Automatic within-lesson advancement}
  \reqshall{Squigglr shall automatically advance from full-outline tracing
to endpoint-only guidance, and from endpoint-only guidance to independent
drawing, upon a passing attempt at the active stage; a passing independent
drawing attempt completes the current lesson without starting a different
lesson.}
  \reqrationale{G.2 and BR-002 require predictable reduction of guidance after
success. Adult consent governs lesson selection, skips and backward moves,
not normal pass-based progression within the selected lesson (CON-007).}
  \reqfit{For a selected lesson, finalize passing attempts at its outline,
endpoint and independent stages; verify the exact transitions T to E, E to I
and I to completed, respectively, without an adult interaction. Verify that
a non-passing attempt stays at its current stage, a different lesson is not
started, and no stage is skipped automatically.}
  \reqpriority{Must}
  \reqphase{PH-1}
  \reqsources{G.2--G.3; BR-002--003; CON-007; \srcref{001}}
  \reqtraces{FR-LRN-013; FR-LRN-016; FR-LRN-018; \ucref{003}; \ucref{004};
OI-04 (TA confirmation)}
\end{srsrequirement}

\subsection{Formal Specification}
\label{sec:fr-formal}

The most safety-relevant learning behaviour is formalized here as a
\emph{labelled transition system}: a set of states and named events with
rules describing when an event changes the state. The model specifies
externally observable outcomes rather than a particular user interface or
scoring algorithm. It covers guidance levels, attempt classification,
optional suggestions, automatic pass-based advancement within a lesson,
and the unsuccessful-attempt stopping rule.
Every variable used below has a type or domain; arrows indicate transitions
\emph{caused by the event above the arrow}, not automatic passage of time.
This notation applies only to guided learning activities.

\paragraph{Domains and state.}
For each selected lesson $\ell$ and learner profile $p$, let
\[
\begin{aligned}
L &= \{D,T,E,I\}, & A &= \{T,E,I\},\\
R &= \{\textsf{complete},\textsf{incomplete},\textsf{invalid}\},\\
M &= \{\textsf{assisted},\textsf{independent}\},\\
Q &= \{\textsf{active},\textsf{suggested},\textsf{ended}\}.
\end{aligned}
\]
The levels $D,T,E,I$ mean \emph{demonstration}, full-outline
\emph{tracing}, \emph{endpoint-only} and \emph{independent} drawing,
respectively. Only levels in $A$ admit scored drawing attempts; $D$ is a
non-scored demonstration. A state is
\[
\sigma=(\ell,p,l,q,n_f,n_a,k,H,s,z,x,f),
\]
where $l\in L$ is the current guidance level of the adult-selected lesson,
$q\in Q$ is the session status, $n_f\in\mathbb{N}_0$ counts non-passing
evaluable attempts at this stage, $n_a\in\mathbb{N}_0$ counts finalized,
evaluable attempts at this stage, $k\in\mathbb{N}_0$ is the consecutive
